# Volume 01 Knowledge Graph Candidate Registry

## Purpose

This registry defines the complete candidate relationship inventory for all 101
Volume 01 Concepts while preserving the operational KOS edge registry as the
atomic authority.

## Scope

It covers Concept topology, curriculum practice and assessment edges, incoming
and outgoing adjacency, direct and indirect prerequisites, and learning-path
mappings. No visual graph or educational content is produced.

## Ownership

- **Document authority:** Derived candidate inventory.
- **Atomic edge authority:** [`knowledge/registry/edges/`](../../knowledge/registry/edges/).
- **Concept source:** [Concept Candidate Registry](./concept-registry.md), version `1.0.0`.
- **Graph standard:** `ASEA-STD-KOS-007`, version `1.1.0`.
- **Generated:** 2026-07-18.
- **Existing Approved operational edges:** 10.
- **New Candidate edge reservations:** 730.
